### [105] [Thin-Shell Wormholes from Entropy-Induced Black-Hole Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14130)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于修正黑洞熵构造对称薄壳虫洞，将Darmois-Israel衔接条件用熵导数重新表述，分析了八种熵方案。结果表明对称构造要求负表面能量密度，常压分支线性不稳定，而变Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性取决于熵几何与喉部物质动力学的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立从修正黑洞熵出发统一构造薄壳虫洞的理论框架，探究不同熵方案对虫洞几何和稳定性的影响。

Method: 从通用熵延迟函数出发构造对称截剪虫洞，将Darmois-Israel衔接条件用熵导数重新表述，推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒方程和径向有效势，应用于Bekenstein-Hawking、Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto、Rényi、Kaniadakis、对数、圈量子引力及指数等八种熵方案。

Result: 对称构造在允许的正延迟域内要求负表面能量密度；熵变形可显著改变视界结构、允许喉部区域和表面应力分布；所有常压分支在线性径向扰动下均不稳定；变Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性由熵几何、喉部半径和壳层状态方程径向指数共同决定。

Conclusion: 建立了比较熵致黑洞几何作为薄壳虫洞种子的统一框架，表明稳定性并非仅由熵变形单独决定，而是取决于熵与喉部局域物质动力学响应的相互作用。

Abstract: Modified black-hole entropies can induce effective spacetime geometries and thereby provide a thermodynamic route for investigating thin-shell wormholes. In this work, we construct symmetric cut-and-paste wormholes from the generic entropic lapse function $F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$ and formulate the Darmois--Israel junction conditions directly in terms of the lapse and of the entropy derivatives. We derive the surface stresses, shell energy-condition combinations, conservation equation, and radial effective potential, and then apply the formalism to the Bekenstein--Hawking, Barrow, Tsallis--Cirto, Rényi, Kaniadakis, logarithmic, loop-quantum-gravity-inspired, and exponential entropy prescriptions. The analysis shows that the symmetric construction requires negative surface energy density throughout every admissible positive-lapse domain, although entropy deformations can significantly modify the horizon structure, the allowed throat region, and the localization of the surface stresses. Within the parameter domains considered here, all examined constant-barotropic branches are linearly radially unstable, despite quantitative changes in their near-horizon scales. In contrast, a variable Chaplygin shell can support stable configurations, with the stability domains determined jointly by the entropic geometry, the throat radius, and the radial exponent of the shell equation of state. These results establish a unified framework for comparing entropy-induced black-hole geometries as thin-shell wormhole seeds and show that stability is governed not by the entropy deformation alone, but by its interplay with the dynamical response of the matter localized at the throat.

</details>


### [106] [A note on the conformal geometry of the 2-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14133)
*Jörg Frauendiener*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究二维球面上的共形因子如何将给定度规变为圆度量规，发现存在唯一的对称无迹张量补充Ricci张量构成无散对称张量，由此导出具有Minkowski结构的线性二阶PDE，并证明存在无穷多个由"4-向量"参数化的相关空间，最后证明每个2-球面都能规范地等距嵌入Minkowski空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从共形几何的角度理解二维球面的几何结构，特别是共形因子将给定度规变为圆度规的几何意义，以及球面与Minkowski空间之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过构造唯一对称无迹张量补充Ricci张量以获得无散对称张量，进而推导线性二阶偏微分方程，分析其解空间的Minkowski结构，并利用等距参数化方法研究无穷多相关空间，最后构建规范等距嵌入。

Result: 得到了具有Minkowski结构的线性二阶PDE的解空间，发现无穷多个由"4-向量"参数化的等距相关空间，并实现了每个2-球面到Minkowski空间的规范等距嵌入。

Conclusion: 二维球面的共形几何与Minkowski空间存在深刻联系，球面可视为规范嵌入到Minkowski空间中，这一结构由无穷多个等距族参数化。

Abstract: We discuss the geometry of the two-dimensional sphere. Our perspective focuses on the set of conformal factors that rescale a given metric to a round metric. We find that there exists a unique symmetric and trace-free tensor on the sphere which complements the Ricci tensor to a divergence-free symmetric tensor. Using this tensor, we derive a linear second-order PDE whose solution space carries a natural Minkowski structure. We show that there are in fact infinitely many such spaces, all related by isometries which can be thought of as being parametrised by ``4-vectors''. Finally, we show that every 2-sphere can be isometrically embedded in a Minkowski space in a canonical way.

</details>


### [107] [An effective Friedmann equation for a bouncing anisotropic universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14153)
*Syed Moeez Hassan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文针对反弹聚合物量子化Bianchi-I时空，推导了广义有效弗里德曼方程和雷乔杜里方程，并精确证明了文献中数值推导出的物质能量密度与各向异性剪切在反弹时刻的关系对任何物质类型都成立，同时给出了该关系中常数的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子宇宙学中，理解反弹时刻物质能量密度与各向异性剪切之间的关系对于描述早期宇宙的各向异性行为至关重要。此前文献中这一关系仅通过数值方法获得，缺乏理论上的严格证明，且其适用性和常数表达式尚不明确。

Method: 论文采用聚合物量子化的方法处理Bianchi-I时空，这是一种基于圈量子宇宙学（LQC）的非微扰量子化方案。通过推导广义有效弗里德曼方程和雷乔杜里方程，作者在分析层面建立了反弹时刻物质能量密度与各向异性剪切之间的精确关系。

Result: 成功得到了推广的有效动力学方程，并严格证明了反弹时刻物质能量密度与各向异性剪切平方之间存在线性关系（ρ ∝ σ²），且该关系对所有物质组分均成立。给出了该关系中比例常数的精确解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子引力效应在早期宇宙各向异性演化中的角色提供了更坚实的理论基础，表明反弹点的物理性质（如最大能量密度对剪切的依赖性）具有普适性，不受物质类型影响，为后续研究量子反弹宇宙学模型提供了重要依据。

Abstract: We derive generalized effective Friedmann and Raychaudhuri equations for a bouncing polymer quantized Bianchi-I spacetime. We further prove that the relation numerically derived in the literature between the matter energy density and the anisotropic shear at the bounce holds exactly for any type of matter content, and derive explicit expressions for the constants that appear in that relation.

</details>


### [108] [Reconstructions of Horndeski Subclasses by Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14154)
*Marco C. Mendoza Marcos,Ana A. Avilez Lopez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，从宇宙计时器和重子声学振荡的观测数据中重建了Horndeski理论的两个子类（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon），发现Brans-Dicke子类在红移0.4-1.7范围内优于ΛCDM模型，而Cubic Galileon子类在整个观测红移范围内都支持标量场与引力的修正及随时间演化的暗能量密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Horndeski理论框架下，利用多种晚期宇宙学观测数据，对两个子类理论（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon）进行重建，以检验这些修正引力理论是否比ΛCDM模型更好地描述宇宙演化，并探究标量场与引力非最小耦合导致的引力常数时间变化。

Method: 使用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，结合宇宙计时器（独立于模型的哈勃率测量）和重子声学振荡（哈勃率及距离测量）数据，对Horndeski理论中的Brans-Dicke子类和Cubic Galileon子类进行反演重建。这些理论已满足引力波传播速度的约束条件。

Result: Brans-Dicke子类的最可能重建在红移0.4-1.7范围内优于ΛCDM模型；Cubic Galileon子类的最可能重建在整个观测红移范围内均支持标量场与引力的修正，且具有随时间演化的暗能量密度。

Conclusion: 数据倾向于支持Horndeski理论的修正引力解释，而非标准ΛCDM模型，尤其是在中等红移范围，这表明需要超越宇宙学常数模型的物理机制来描述晚期宇宙加速膨胀。

Abstract: In this work we reconstruct two subclasses of theories from the Horndeski class, the most general scalar-tensor theories of gravity with second-order equations, according to a variety of late-universe cosmological data: model independent measurements of Hubble rate from Cosmic Chronometers, as well as Hubble rate and distance measurements from Baryonic Acoustic Oscillations. In the modified gravity family considered, the scalar field is non-minimally coupled to gravity leading to time variations in the gravitational constant at cosmological scales. The theories considered are also consistent with constraints on the propagation speed of gravitational waves. The reconstruction is carried out by using the Gaussian Processes bayesian technique either for the Brans-Dicke and the Cubic Galileon subclasses of Horndeski theory. We find that the most likely Gaussian Process reconstruction for the data favors a cosmology from the Brans-Dicke subclass over $Λ$CDM in the range $z \in (0.4, 1.7)$, and for the Cubic Galileon subclass we find that the most likely reconstruction indeed favors modifications of the scalar field to gravity with time evolving dark energy density in the whole range of redshift observations.

</details>


### [109] [Breaking the Degeneracy: Spectral Hardening of Accretion Disks around Rotating Hayward Black Holes in Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14164)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了嵌入宏观暗物质晕的自转规则Hayward黑洞的薄吸积盘热力学与观测特征，发现量子修正核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率具有叠加增强效应（自旋$j=0.8$时可达$\sim14.4\%$），但几何修正（Hayward真空）与天体物理修正（Kerr+暗物质）在低频区间存在宏观简并性，仅在高频Wien尾端可被打破，为未来高频光谱偏振测量区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境提供了关键诊断依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑洞吸积盘模型多基于经典Kerr解或单独考虑暗物质环境，但缺乏对量子修正规则黑洞与暗物质晕共存场景的系统研究。特别是，非奇异黑洞与暗物质在观测特征上可能产生不可区分的简并，亟需建立区分两者的诊断方法。

Method: 构建三区域复合时空模型：内区为de Sitter极限规则化的Hayward核心，中间为指数球密度分布的暗物质壳层（无压尘埃包络），外区为渐近平坦真空。通过质量分布匹配各区域，计算ISCO、辐射效率、局域热流及多色黑体谱光度，系统比较Kerr真空、Hayward真空、Kerr+DM、Hayward+DM四种构型。

Result: 发现ISCO存在严格层次压缩；量子核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率产生叠加增强效应（高自旋$j=0.8$时达$\sim14.4\%$）；几何修正（Hayward真空）与天体物理修正（Kerr+DM）在低中频段存在宏观简并性。

Conclusion: 该简并性仅在极端Wien尾端的高频波段被结构性地打破，因此未来高频光谱偏振测量可作为区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境的关键诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and observable signatures of thin accretion disks surrounding rotating, regular Hayward black holes embedded within a macroscopic dark matter (DM) envelope. The spacetime is rigorously modelled as a three-region composite: an inner Hayward core regularised by a de~Sitter limit, an intermediate DM shell modelled as a pressureless dust envelope governed by an exponential sphere density profile, and an asymptotically flat outer vacuum. By matching these regions via the mass profile, we compute the explicit modifications to the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO), radiative efficiency, local thermal flux, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity. A systematic comparison across four distinct configurations (Kerr vacuum, Hayward vacuum, Kerr\,+\,DM, and Hayward\,+\,DM) reveals a strict hierarchical compression of the ISCO. We demonstrate that the quantum-inspired core regularity and the macroscopic DM halo exert an additive enhancement on the radiative efficiency, reaching $\sim 14.4\%$ for highly spinning black holes ($j=0.8$). However, this additive behaviour introduces a profound macroscopic degeneracy between purely geometric (Hayward vacuum) and purely astrophysical (Kerr\,+\,DM) configurations at low-to-intermediate frequencies. We establish that this degeneracy is structurally broken only in the extreme Wien tail of the multi-wavelength spectrum, providing a critical diagnostic footprint for future high-frequency spectropolarimetry to distinguish non-singular black holes from classical dark matter environments.

</details>


### [110] [Probing Quantum Gravity through Chaotic Orbits and Strong-Field Effects in Kerr Black Holes Embedded in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14170)
*Shubham Kala,Sara Saghafi,M. Yousaf,Hemwati Nandan,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Chengxun Yuan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过多种非线性动力学方法（Poincaré截面、Lyapunov指数、KS熵和加权Birkhoff平均）研究了量子改进旋转黑洞在完美流体暗物质（PFDM）背景下的光子动力学，发现量子修正参数和PFDM参数会增强混沌行为，显著改变有效势，导致规则与混沌区域共存的混合相空间结构，并对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜具有潜在观测意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子引力修正和完美流体暗物质对旋转黑洞附近光子非线性动力学的联合影响，以及这些效应对圆形轨道稳定性和相空间结构的作用，旨在理解量子与暗物质贡献在强引力场中的角色。

Method: 采用Poincaré截面分析相空间结构转变；Lyapunov指数量化轨道对初始条件的敏感性；Kolmogorov-Sinai熵度量动力学复杂性；加权Birkhoff平均提供区分规则与混沌轨道的定量判据。

Result: 量子改进参数和PFDM参数增大时，不变环面变形碎裂、相空间出现散射混沌区域；Lyapunov指标显示两者均增强光子轨道对初始条件的敏感性；KS熵证实混沌行为随参数增长；WBA实现了相空间结构的详细映射。

Conclusion: 量子引力修正与暗物质的联合效应显著改变光子运动的有效势，产生规则与混沌共存的丰富混合相空间结构，对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜的观测研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear photon dynamics in quantum improved rotating black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) using several methods of analysis, including Poincaré sections, Lyapunov exponents, Kolmogorov-Sinai (KS) entropy and weighted Birkhoff averages (WBA). In particular, we examine the effect of quantum-improved parameter $(\tildeω)$ and PFDM parameter $(ζ)$ on the null geodesic motion hence stability of circular orbit. Poincaré sections illustrate the transition from regular to chaotic motion as these parameters increase, characterized by the deformation and fragmentation of invariant tori and the emergence of scattered chaotic regions in phase space. The stability properties of null circular orbits are quantified through the Lyapunov indicators, revealing that both the quantum improvement parameter and the PFDM parameter enhance the sensitivity of photon trajectories to initial conditions. The KS entropy provides an independent measure of dynamical complexity and confirms the growth of chaotic behavior with increasing quantum and PFDM corrections. Additionally, the WBA method offers a robust quantitative criterion for distinguishing regular and chaotic orbits and allows a detailed mapping of the phase-space structure. The results demonstrate that the combined effects of quantum gravity corrections and PFDM significantly modify the effective potential governing photon motion, leading to a rich mixed phase-space structure with coexisting regular and chaotic regions. These findings underscore the crucial role of quantum and dark matter contributions in shaping photon dynamics near rotating black holes and suggest possible observational implications on black hole shadows and gravitational lensing in strong-field regimes.

</details>


### [111] [Novel topological subclass in Bardeen-AdS-class black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14173)
*Yu-Die Wan,Peng Zhao,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于φ-映射拓扑流理论，系统研究了与非线性电动力学耦合的规则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞的热力学拓扑，在W^{0-}拓扑族中发现了一个新的内次级子类\widetilde{W}^{0-}，该子类具有零全局拓扑荷W=0和内外视界绕数签名[-,+]，并通过两个临界耦合参数划分了不同的拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞（奇点自由的几何结构）的拓扑分类尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究其热力学拓扑结构。

Method: 基于φ-映射拓扑流理论，对两个典型AdS半径(L=1和L=15)进行数值计算，通过分析全局拓扑荷W=0及内外视界绕数签名[-,+]，识别出W^{0-}族内部的新子类\widetilde{W}^{0-}。

Result: 识别出新的内次级子类\widetilde{W}^{0-}（全局拓扑荷W=0，绕数签名[-,+]）；发现两个临界耦合参数\hat{m}_{01}和\hat{m}_{02}将参数空间划分为不同拓扑相：Type I(m_0≥\hat{m}_{01})和Type II(\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01})属于\widetilde{W}^{0-}，而0<m_0≤\hat{m}_{02}属于传统W^{0-}。

Conclusion: 数值结果验证了拓扑形式对无奇点规则黑洞的自洽性，精炼了针对非线性电动力学规则黑洞的拓扑判别准则，为后续在相同形式下研究高维旋转规则黑洞的拓扑性质提供了理论参考。

Abstract: Based on the $φ$-mapping topological current theory, we systematically investigate the thermodynamic topology of regular Bardeen-AdS-class black holes coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, a class of singularity-free geometries whose topological classification remains underexplored. We implement numerical calculations at two typical AdS radii $L=1$ and $L=15$. Within the fundamental $W^{0-}$ topological family, we identify a novel inner secondary subclass $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$. This result only refines and enriches the internal branch structure of the $W^{0-}$ family under the established classification scheme of five topological classes and four topological subclasses for black hole thermodynamics. This new subclass has a vanishing global topological charge $W=0$ with an inner-outer horizon winding-number signature $[-,+]$. Unlike standard $W^{0-}$ solutions that realize topological extension via embedded $(+,-)$ winding pairs, $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$ arises from attaching an extra stable branch to the end of the base solution sequence. Two critical coupling parameters $\hat{m}_{01}$ and $\hat{m}_{02}$ divide the full parameter space into distinct topological phases: Type I black holes with $m_0\ge\hat{m}_{01}$ and Type II solutions with $\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01}$ belong to $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$, while the parameter region $0<m_0\le\hat{m}_{02}$ corresponds to the conventional $W^{0-}$ topology. Our numerical results verify the self-consistency of the thermodynamic topological formalism when applied to singularity-free regular black holes, refine the topological discrimination criteria tailored to nonlinear-electrodynamics regular black holes, and provide theoretical references for subsequent topological investigations of higher-dimensional rotating regular black holes under the same formalism.

</details>


### [112] [Coupled by Design: Computing Kerr-Newman Quasinormal Modes with a Hybrid SpectralPINN Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14216)
*Alexandre M. Pombo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将SpectralPINN求解器从可分离的Kerr黑洞扩展到耦合的Kerr-Newman系统，求解两个耦合主方程，计算引力-电磁耦合下的类光球和近地平线拟正规模式，首次系统计算了矢量主导的类光球分支，并基于公开数据集验证了高精度（最差10⁻⁴，通常10⁻⁷），进而分析了特征值排斥现象及对Einstein望远镜约束电荷质量比的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SpectralPINN从可分离的单方程Kerr情况推进到真正耦合的两场问题（Kerr-Newman），以求解引力场与电磁场耦合下的拟正规模式。

Method: 扩展SpectralPINN求解器，求解两个耦合的主偏微分方程系统，数值计算Kerr-Newman黑洞的拟正规模频率。

Result: 相对频率误差最差约10⁻⁴，多数情况约10⁻⁷；公开数据集涵盖ℓ≤4的五个类光球模（引力主导和矢量主导）及两个基本近地平线引力主导模；首次系统计算并表征了矢量主导的类光球分支。

Conclusion: 验证了SpectralPINN处理耦合PDE系统的能力；观察到特征值排斥现象 onset；排除分辨域内的跨偏振排斥；为Einstein望远镜约束黑洞电荷质量比提供了预测基础。

Abstract: We extend our \texttt{SpectralPINN} solver to the computation of Kerr-Newman quasinormal modes by applying it to solve the system of two coupled master PDEs -- advancing from the single, separable equation of the uncharged Kerr limit to a genuinely coupled two-field problem. The coupling between the gravitational and electromagnetic fields gives rise to two families of solutions: the photon-sphere, connecting with Kerr; and near-horizon, disconnected from the Kerr limit, each with two branches of solutions depending on the leading field: the gravitational- and vector-led. Benchmarking against publicly available datasets shows relative frequency errors of $\sim 10^{-4}$ worst case and $\sim10^{-7}$ for most cases. The computed public dataset spans five photon-sphere modes, both gravitational- and vector-led, up to $\ell=4$, as well as two fundamental gravitational-led near-horizon modes. The vector-led photon-sphere branch is computed and systematically characterized for the first time. We apply the dataset and observe the onset of the eigenvalue repulsion reported by Dias \textit{et al.}, to exclude an inter-polarization repulsion within the resolved domain, and to forecast Einstein Telescope constraints on the black-hole charge-to-mass ratio.

</details>


### [113] [Acceleration radiation and HBAR thermodynamics for atoms falling into a BTZ black hole: A CQM quantum-optics approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14281)
*H. E. Camblong,A. Chakraborty,C. R. Ordóñez,M. O. Scully,G. Valdivia-Mera,H. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware-like真空中会发射具有Hawking温度$T_H$的普朗克谱辐射。对于随机初始时间制备的原子云，会产生热Hawking-like辐射。该辐射与相对等效原理相关。尽管(2+1)维引力的拓扑性质，BTZ黑洞的热力学和辐射性质仍由近地平线共形量子力学(CQM)普适支配。这种共轭行为产生与光子辐射场相关的HBAR熵$S_{\mathcal P}$，它模仿了Bekenstein-Hawking熵$S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$，且具有正确的1/4比例因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware-like真空中的辐射行为，验证其是否产生Hawking-like辐射，并揭示该现象与相对等效原理及近地平线共形量子力学的关系。

Method: 采用量子光学方法，将每个原子解释为探测器，分析原子-场相互作用；通过共形量子力学(CQM)框架研究BTZ黑洞的热力学和辐射性质。

Result: 自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子发射具有Hawking温度$T_H$的普朗克谱辐射；随机初始时间制备的原子云产生热Hawking-like辐射；发现与光子辐射场相关的HBAR熵$S_{\mathcal P}$模仿Bekenstein-Hawking熵$S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$，且具有正确的1/4比例因子。

Conclusion: BTZ黑洞的辐射性质与高维黑洞一样由近地平线共形量子力学普适支配，验证了相对等效原理在该过程中的作用，并揭示了辐射场熵与黑洞熵之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Atoms falling freely into a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole in a Boulware-like vacuum are shown to emit radiation with a Planck spectrum at the Hawking temperature $T_{H}$. This leads to thermal Hawking-like radiation for a cloud of falling atoms prepared with random initial times. Moreover, the radiation is related to the relative equivalence principle, with the vacuum field modes accelerated with respect to the falling atom. The physics of the atom-field interactions is most easily described within a quantum optics approach, where each atom can be interpreted as a detector. Despite the topological nature of gravity in $(2+1)$ dimensions, the thermodynamic and radiation properties of BTZ black holes are still universally governed by the same near-horizon conformal quantum mechanics (CQM) applicable to higher-dimensional gravity. This universal conformal behavior is exhibited by all fields in the background of generic black holes, and generates an HBAR entropy $S_{\mathcal P}$ associated with the photon radiation field that mimics the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$, proportional to the black-hole horizon area, and with the correct $1/4$ proportionality factor.

</details>


### [114] [Upwind embedded boundary SBP operators: New high order numerical schemes for arbitrarily shaped domains with Cartesian grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14403)
*Conner Dailey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在嵌入式边界求和分部（SBP）方法框架下，推导了新型差分算子，提高了内部和边界闭合的精度阶数，同时最小化边界误差。通过推广至具有更好色散关系保持特性的迎风格式，改善了网格谱性质，并在3D多块网格上通过曲线标量波动方程验证了算子的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入式边界SBP方法已用于黑洞剥离模拟，但边界误差和谱性质仍有改进空间。本文旨在推导更高阶精度的算子，同时通过迎风推广改善波方程的色散关系保持特性，提升非线性问题的求解鲁棒性。

Method: 在嵌入式边界SBP框架下，推导新的插值/外插算子，提高内部和边界闭合的精度阶数并最小化边界误差；进一步将算子推广至迎风格式，优化频谱特性。

Result: 在3D多块网格上，对中心块嵌入剥离球面的曲线标量波动方程进行测试，验证了新算子的鲁棒性和高精度。

Conclusion: 新算子实现了更高阶精度的边界闭合与更优的谱性质，为非线性问题（如黑洞模拟）提供了更可靠的嵌入式边界SBP方法。

Abstract: Embedded boundary summation by parts (SBP) methods define finite differencing based derivative operators with the added feature that the boundary need not coincide with a grid cell, allowing a boundary to be embedded on a regular Cartesian grid. This is achieved by the introduction of interpolation/extrapolation operators that match the accuracy of the boundary closure. These methods have been used to perform black hole excision simulations on a domain with a spherical boundary embedded in a regular Cartesian grid, demonstrating their usefulness for nonlinear problems. In this work, new operators are derived using this embedded boundary framework to increase the order of accuracy of the interior and boundary closure while minimizing the boundary error. Additionally, these novel operators improve the spectral properties on the grid by generalizing to an upwind scheme that has better dispersion relation preserving properties compared to traditional SBP schemes for wave equations. These operators are tested with the curvilinear scalar wave equation on a 3D multiblock grid with an excision sphere embedded in the center block to demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of these novel embedded operators.

</details>


### [115] [Warped Spacelike Singularities and the $C^0$-Inextendibility of Birmingham-Kottler Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14741)
*Bobby EkaGunara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对一类翘曲类空奇点，建立了连续洛伦兹延拓的局部障碍，并证明了一类Birmingham-Kottler时空的C⁰不可延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究翘曲类空奇点处连续洛伦兹延拓的局部障碍，并解决特定Birmingham-Kottler时空族的C⁰不可延拓性问题。

Method: 通过径向压缩终端因果迹线，构造紧凑时间分离子；结合纵向平移产生内在发散但延拓图中有界控制的径向切片距离；利用测地线分类迫使边界趋近的有限极大化子到达奇异端点。

Result: 得到了由两个可积条件、相对翘曲因子的单调性条件以及纵向因子发散性表达的局部障碍判据；证明了一类单视界Birmingham-Kottler时空（非正宇宙学常数，闭纤维满足Ric_γΣ=(n-2)kγΣ）的C⁰不可延拓性。

Conclusion: 无需纤维的齐次性、可定向性或单连通性假设，即可建立该族时空的C⁰不可延拓性。

Abstract: We establish a local obstruction to continuous Lorentzian extensions at a class of warped spacelike singularities. The criterion is expressed through two integrability conditions, a monotonicity condition on the relative warp factors, and divergence of the longitudinal factor; it does not require any symmetry of the closed fiber. The main geometric step is a radial compression of terminal causal traces, which replaces the rotational deformation available in spherical symmetry. The compression yields a compact chronological separator in an adapted boundary chart. Longitudinal translations then produce radial-slice distances that diverge intrinsically while remaining uniformly controlled in the extension chart. Next, we construct the canonical one-horizon Birmingham-Kottler spacetime in global Kruskal coordinates and classify all finite proper-time ends of its timelike geodesics. Every boundary-approaching finite maximizer supplied by a putative extension is thereby forced to the singular end. The local obstruction and the geodesic classification imply $C^0$-inextendibility for the one-horizon Birmingham-Kottler family with nonpositive cosmological constant and every closed connected fiber satisfying $\text{Ric}_{γ_Σ}=(n-2)kγ_Σ$, without assumptions of homogeneity, orientability, or simple connectivity.

</details>


### [116] [Scalarization of Charged Black Hole in Gauss-Bonnet Extended Starobinsky-Maxwell Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14620)
*Rui-Xi Zhu,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Starobinsky引力中补充高斯-邦内特不变量的黑洞自发标量化现象，发现了此前未被报道的多分支结构：静态球对称标量化解分裂为两个光滑分支，在共同的最小视界半径处连接。引入麦克斯韦场后，该多分支结构持续存在，并在特定参数范围内出现新的额外不连通分支。研究详细分析了从单个光滑分支到两个不连通分支的转变，并验证了所有带电毛状解精确满足第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Starobinsky引力中补充高斯-邦内特不变量的黑洞自发标量化现象，探索是否存在新的解结构特征。

Method: 通过理论分析重新考察Starobinsky引力框架下的标量化方程，引入麦克斯韦场扩展理论，系统研究解的分支结构及其转变过程，并使用标准麦克斯韦热力学关系验证第一定律。

Result: 发现了此前未被报道的多分支结构：静态球对称标量化解分裂为两个光滑分支，在共同的最小视界半径处连接；引入麦克斯韦场后该结构持续存在，并在特定参数范围内出现新的额外不连通分支。

Conclusion: Starobinsky引力中补充高斯-邦内特不变量的黑洞自发标量化存在多分支结构，所有带电毛状解精确满足第一定律。

Abstract: We re-examine spontaneous scalarization of black holes within Starobinsky gravity supplemented by the Gauss-Bonnet invariant. A novel feature is uncovered: scalarized solutions split into two smooth branches that connect at a common minimal horizon radius, a multi-branch structure previously unreported for static, spherically symmetric scalarization. Extending the theory with a Maxwell field, we find that this multi-branch structure persists, while a new additional disconnected branch emerges within certain parameter ranges. We thoroughly analyse the transition from a single smooth branch to two disconnected branches. Lastly, we verify, employing the standard Maxwell thermodynamic relations, that all charged hairy solutions obey the first law exactly.

</details>


### [117] [Random walks around black holes and low-frequency X-ray variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14626)
*Arthur G. Suvorov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在弯曲时空（黑洞背景）中描述湍流流体中颗粒随机游走的模型，通过求解Fokker-Planck方程发现，相比平直空间，颗粒在黑洞附近的逃逸概率反而更高，并研究了由此产生的X射线变率谱特征，与天鹅座X-1的观测相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解强引力场中吸积过程的固有随机性，传统模型常忽略弯曲时空几何对颗粒随机动力学的影响，作者希望建立一种曲率加权的随机游走框架来更准确地描述这一过程。

Method: 将嵌入湍流流体中的颗粒在强引力场中的随机动力学形式化为黎曼流形上的随机游走；求解黑洞背景下的Fokker-Planck方程；模拟大量初始分布在薄盘结构方程所设定密度轮廓的同心壳层上的颗粒轨迹。

Result: 发现黑洞背景下颗粒的逃逸概率高于平直空间，这是因为视界附近径向胞的拉伸提供了更大的空间体积供颗粒游走；低频段谱指数较平直空间更浅（时间膨胀所致），高频段更陡；Schwarzschild加权的谱与天鹅座X-1硬态的低频X射线变率观测 broadly 吻合。

Conclusion: 几何漂移效应可能在描述随机吸积过程中扮演重要角色，弯曲时空的随机行走模型能够解释观测到的低频X射线变率特征。

Abstract: The stochastic dynamics of a grain embedded within a turbulent fluid subject to strong gravitational fields can be formulated as a random walk on a Riemannian manifold. Such curvature-weighted walks provide a framework to model the intrinsic variability of accretion onto compact objects. By solving the relevant Fokker-Planck equation on a black hole background, we find the counterintuitive result that the escape probability of a grain is actually higher compared to flat space. This is a consequence of the stretching of radial cells near the event horizon: there is a greater spatial volume for the particle to wander through before being captured. By simulating a large number of grain trajectories, initially distributed on concentric shells with a density profile set by the thin-disc structure equations, we also study particle fluxes through the horizon. Shallower spectral indices emerge at low frequencies relative to flat space, primarily due to time dilation, and steeper ones at high frequencies. We find that Schwarzschild-weighted spectra broadly match observations of low-frequency X-ray variability from systems like Cygnus X-1 in their hard state, suggesting that geometric drifts may be important in describing stochastic accretion processes.

</details>


### [118] [Orbital Dynamics and Gravitational-Wave Signatures of EMRIs in Self-Dual Loop Quantum Gravity Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14746)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了圈量子引力修正下的极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统，分析了自对偶LQG黑洞时空中的轨道动力学和引力波信号，发现量子修正会在长旋近过程中累积并导致波形偏离经典情况，并通过Fisher矩阵分析表明未来天基探测器有望约束量子参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子引力（LQG）预测对经典黑洞时空存在量子修正，这些修正可能在强场区域的动力学和引力波信号中留下印记。本文旨在探究EMRI系统作为探测LQG量子引力效应的探针的可行性。

Method: 首先在静态球对称自对偶LQG黑洞时空中分析试验粒子运动，研究有效势和轨道结构；然后基于轨道演化构建引力波波形；接着通过Newman-Janis算法构造旋转推广的LQG修正Kerr几何；最后利用Fisher矩阵分析评估未来天基探测器对量子参数的约束能力。

Result: LQG量子修正改变了近水平轨道动力学，影响圆轨道稳定性和强场轨道行为；量子效应在长旋近过程中累积，导致波形与经典情况出现显著偏差；旋转情况下自旋与量子修正存在相互影响；Fisher分析表明未来天基引力波探测器有望对量子参数提供有意义的约束。

Conclusion: EMRI观测为探测黑洞时空中的量子引力效应提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Loop quantum gravity (LQG) predicts quantum modifications to classical black-hole spacetimes, which may leave imprints on the dynamics and gravitational-wave signals of compact objects in the strong-field regime. In this work, we investigate the orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave signatures of extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in a self-dual loop quantum gravity black hole spacetime. We analyze test-particle motion in the static, spherically symmetric self-dual LQG geometry characterized by two quantum parameters: the polymeric parameter $P$ and the minimal area parameter $a_0$. The effective potential and orbital structure are systematically studied, and we quantify the influence of quantum corrections on circular-orbit stability and strong-field orbital behavior. Compared with the classical Schwarzschild spacetime, LQG corrections modify the near-horizon orbital dynamics. Based on the orbital evolution, we construct gravitational-wave waveforms and investigate the impact of quantum corrections on waveform morphology. We find that LQG effects accumulate during the long inspiral phase, leading to noticeable signal deviations from the classical case. To incorporate rotation, we construct a rotating extension of the self-dual spacetime using the Newman--Janis algorithm. The resulting LQG-corrected Kerr geometry is used to analyze orbital motion, revealing the interplay between spin and quantum corrections in strong-field trajectories. Finally, we perform a Fisher matrix analysis to estimate potential constraints on quantum parameters from future space-based gravitational-wave detectors. Our results indicate that EMRI observations provide a promising avenue to probe quantum gravitational effects in black-hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [119] [The radial action for massive particles in spherically symmetric geometries: Exact resummation at any PM order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14802)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了多种球对称时空（Schwarzschild、Schwarzschild-Tangherlini、D3-膜时空）中沿双曲型测地线的有质量粒子径向作用量，展示了其在eikonal极限下用特殊函数（超几何、Fox-Wright函数）的求和性质，并推广了先前零测地线的结果。散射角因此也可被求和。此外，在4维Schwarzschild黑洞时空中，利用量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法，证明了量子a-圈（重整化角动量）与径向作用量在有大质量情形下存在简单关系，给出了完全求和的表达式。最后展示了a_D-圈的显式展开形式，但尚未得到其求和表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有质量粒子沿双曲型测地线的径向作用量从零质量情形推广到有质量情形，并探索其在多个球对称时空中的求和性质；同时利用量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法将径向问题与量子可积系统联系起来，以得到完全求和的解析结果。

Method: 采用解析计算方法，分别处理4维Schwarzschild时空、d维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini解以及D3-膜时空三种背景。在eikonal极限下引入特殊函数（超几何函数、Fox-Wright函数）实现径向作用量的求和。在4维情形下，将标量场的径向方程映射到量子Seiberg-Witten曲线，通过计算量子a-圈（重整化角动量）得到径向作用量的求和表达式。

Result: 成功得到了有质量粒子在上述多种球对称时空中径向作用量的求和表达式（用超几何/Fox-Wright函数表示），并由此得到散射角的求和形式。在4维Schwarzschild时空中，建立了量子a-圈与径向作用量的简单关系，并给出了完全求和的结果。对于a_D-圈，给出了展开式但未能求和。

Conclusion: 该工作将有质量粒子沿双曲型测地线的径向作用量和散射角从零质量情形推广到有质量情形，实现了在多种球对称时空中的求和表达，并建立了与量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法的联系，为强引力散射问题提供了更精确的解析工具。

Abstract: We compute the massive particles radial action along hyperboliclike geodesics in various spherically symmetric spacetimes: the standard $4d$ Schwarzschild spacetime, its $d$-dimensional genralization known as Schwarzschild-Tangherlini solutions and for the D3-branes spacetimes, showing useful resummation properties in terms of special (hypergeometric, Fox-Wright) functions in the eikonal limit and generalizing previous results valid for null geodesics. As a consequence, the scattering angle can be resummed too, and we explicitly display the resummed expressions.
  In addition, in the more interesting situation of a $4d$ Schwarzschild black hole spacetime, following the approach of the quantum Seiberg-Witten curves to the radial equation associated with a massive scalar field, we show that the quantum $a$-cycle (or, equivalently, the \lq\lq renormalized angular momentum") is simply related to radial action also in this massive case, providing fully resummed expressions. Finally, we display the explicit, expanded-form expression of the dual $a_D$-cycle, for which, however, no resummed expressions have been derived yet.

</details>


### [120] [Chaos bound violation by spinning particles in Gauss-Bonnet-AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14863)
*Xiaowei Li,Mengxuan Wu,Guoping Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gauss-Bonnet反德西特时空中自旋试验粒子对混沌边界的违反，发现Lyapunov指数在五维时空随粒子自旋单调增长，而在八、九维时空呈现非单调行为。Gauss-Bonnet参数通过重塑近视界几何显著调控边界违反，高维时空更易违反混沌边界，时空维度和Gauss-Bonnet参数是修正引力理论中混沌边界有效性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正引力理论（特别是Gauss-Bonnet引力）中自旋试验粒子对混沌边界的违反机制，揭示时空维度和Gauss-Bonnet参数如何调控这一违反行为。

Method: 在Gauss-Bonnet反德西特时空中，分析自旋试验粒子的Lyapunov指数行为，通过改变时空维度（五维、八维、九维）、Gauss-Bonnet参数、粒子自旋及总角动量等参数，系统研究混沌边界的违反规律。

Result: 五维时空中Lyapunov指数随粒子自旋单调增长；八、九维时空呈现先减后增的非单调行为。Gauss-Bonnet参数在五维中使Lyapunov指数偏离表面重力先增后减，在高维中更易违反边界。总角动量增强混沌，但当Gauss-Bonnet参数和黑洞电荷较小时，时空维度主导混沌行为。

Conclusion: 时空维度和Gauss-Bonnet参数是决定修正引力理论中混沌边界有效性的重要因素，高维时空和非平凡Gauss-Bonnet耦合会显著影响混沌边界的违反。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the violation of the chaos bound for spinning test particles in Gauss-Bonnet anti-de Sitter spacetime, focusing on the regulatory roles of the Gauss-Bonnet parameter and spacetime dimensionality. In five-dimensional space time, the Lyapunov exponent grows monotonically with particle spin. In contrast, in eight- and nine-dimensional spacetimes, it exhibits non-monotonic behavior-first decreasing and then increasing-reflecting the nonlinear nature of higher-dimensional tensor couplings. The Gauss-Bonnet parameter significantly modulates the violation by reshaping the near-horizon geometry: in five dimensions, the deviation of the Lyapunov exponent from the surface gravity first increases and then decreases with the Gauss-Bonnet parameter, whereas in the higher dimensions the bound is more easily violated. Increasing the total angular momentum of the particle also enhances chaos; however, when the Gauss-Bonnet parameter and black hole charge are small, the spacetime dimensionality, rather than the angular momentum, dominates. These results establish the spacetime dimensionality and the Gauss-Bonnet parameter as important factors governing the validity of the chaos bound in modified gravity theories.

</details>


### [121] [Kerr black holes without primary hairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14910)
*J Ovalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类由参数 {M, a} 完全表征的正则轴对称黑洞几何族，该族包含Kerr事件视界，并在正则时空、可积奇点时空以及Kerr解之间插值。其主要特点是：(i) 无需 a ≈ M 即可存在准极端构型；(ii) 为从初始正则构型形成Kerr黑洞提供了可能的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一类介于正则时空与Kerr解之间的黑洞几何族，以研究无需近似极端条件即可存在的准极端构型，并为Kerr黑洞形成过程提供解析描述框架。

Method: 通过引入参数 {M, a} 构造一类正则轴对称黑洞几何，该族在保持Kerr事件视界的同时，在正则时空、可积奇点时空及Kerr解之间实现插值。

Result: 该几何族存在准极端构型，且不要求 a ≈ M；同时提供了一个从初始正则构型到Kerr黑洞形成的可能解析框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究非极端条件下的准极端黑洞行为以及Kerr黑洞的形成机制提供了新的理论工具和视角。

Abstract: We present a class of regular axisymmetric black hole geometries fully characterized by the parameters $\{{\cal M},a\}$ and possessing the Kerr event horizon. This family interpolates between regular spacetimes, configurations with integrable singularities, and the Kerr solution as a limiting case. Its main features are: (i) the existence of quasi-extremal configurations without requiring $a \approx {\cal M}$; and (ii) a possible framework toward an analytical description of Kerr black hole formation from an initially regular configuration.

</details>


### [122] [Comment on "Non-linear interactions in cosmologies with energy exchange"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15012)
*Naman Soni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对《Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 120 (2020)》一文中宇宙学模型的数学推导进行了批判性重新评估，指出了原始文章中的多个数学错误，包括Liénard型方程的简化缺陷、积分常数的不当省略以及参数变易法的错误使用，并通过精确解析解证明修正后的数学框架与原始文章的结论根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正原始文章中存在的数学不一致性，包括Liénard型方程的简化错误、积分常数的无故省略以及参数变易法的不当应用，从而澄清含能量交换宇宙学模型的正确数学框架。

Method: 通过精确推导原始模型中的解析解，识别并纠正原始文章中的数学错误，包括重新审视Liénard型方程的简化过程、恢复被忽略的积分常数、以及正确应用参数变易法。

Result: 修正后的精确解析解表明，原始文章中的数学推导存在根本性缺陷，其声称的结论在正确的数学框架下无法成立。

Conclusion: 原始文章中的数学推导存在多处严重错误，修正后的数学框架与原文结论根本矛盾，因此原文关于含能量交换宇宙学模型的结论不可靠。

Abstract: This work provides a critical reassessment of the cosmological models presented in the article "Non-linear interactions in cosmologies with energy exchange" Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 120 (2020) arXiv:1907.06410 . It points out and corrects several mathematical inconsistencies in the original article. These include a flawed simplification of a Liénard-type equation, unjustified omissions of integration constants, and an incorrect use of the variation-of parameters method. By deriving the exact analytical solutions, it is shown that the corrected mathematical framework fundamentally contradicts the claims of the original article.

</details>


### [123] [Noncommutative black holes: Topological bulk-boundary correspondence and Binary Merger Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15150)
*Ankit Anand,Anshul Mishra,Aditya Singh,Saeed Noori Gashti,Neeraj Kumar,Phongpichit Channuie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过微扰展开和数值分析，研究了非对易时空中带电AdS黑洞的热力学拓扑结构，发现非对易效应显著改变了标准Reissner-Nordström AdS黑洞的热力学行为，并证明了体-边界描述的全局热力学拓扑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非对易时空中带电AdS黑洞的精确临界热力学解析解不可得，作者希望通过微扰方法和数值验证来研究其热力学性质，并利用广义离壳自由能框架探索相空间的拓扑结构。

Method: 采用Lorentzian涂抹物质分布的非对易时空模型，对非对易参数进行微扰展开并数值验证；利用广义离壳自由能框架计算拓扑结构与缠绕数，并分析第二定律对剩余质量的下界约束。

Result: 非对易效应导致热力学行为相对标准RN-AdS黑洞出现定性修改；体描述与边界描述的全局热力学拓扑结构完全一致；非对易修正改变了关键热力学量，尤其是熵和最终黑洞质量。

Conclusion: 非对易时空修正为黑洞热力学引入了新的拓扑特征，且体-边界拓扑对应关系的成立为全息对偶提供了进一步证据；同时非对易效应对黑洞蒸发终态（剩余质量）有重要影响。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic topology of charged AdS black holes in a non-commutative spacetime sourced by Lorentzian-smeared matter distributions. Since exact analytical solutions for the critical thermodynamic quantities are not available, we employ a perturbative expansion in the non-commutative parameter and validate the resulting expressions through numerical analysis. Using the generalized off-shell free-energy framework, we explore the topological structure of the thermodynamic phase space and evaluate the corresponding winding number that characterizes the phase transitions. Our results reveal that non-commutative effects introduce qualitative modifications to the thermodynamic behavior compared with the standard Reissner-Nordström AdS black hole. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the bulk and boundary descriptions possess an identical global thermodynamic topology, providing strong evidence for the correspondence between their topological structures. We also investigate the lower bound on the remnant mass implied by the second law of black-hole thermodynamics and observe that non-commutative corrections modify key thermodynamic quantities, with particular emphasis on the entropy and the final black-hole mass.

</details>


### [124] [Nonlinear Gravitational Wave Interactions in an Expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15170)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom,Zhi-Peng Peng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究在膨胀宇宙数值模拟中探究引力波间的非线性相互作用，发现能量从初始谱峰向长短波方向级联，且级联行为符合四波散射湍流模型的振幅标度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在膨胀宇宙背景下，引力波之间的非线性相互作用（如湍流级联）尚未被充分理解，尤其是在宇宙体积显著增大的动态环境中，现有理论预测（如四波散射模型）需要数值验证。

Method: 在空间体积增大一个数量级以上的宇宙学模型中进行数值模拟，演化随机引力波系综，分析不同振幅下的能谱演化，并与四波散射湍流模型的理论预测进行对比。

Result: 引力波能谱从初始峰值波长向长波和短波方向发生能量级联；不同振幅下的级联行为符合四波散射引力波湍流模型的振幅标度律。

Conclusion: 膨胀宇宙中引力波的非线性相互作用确实会产生湍流级联现象，且该过程可由四波散射模型有效描述，这为理解早期宇宙引力波动力学提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This study explores the nonlinear interactions between gravitational waves in an expanding universe. Random ensembles of gravitational waves are evolved numerically in cosmological models whose spatial volumes increase by more than an order of magnitude during these simulations. These evolutions show the spectra of the gravitational waves evolving as energy cascades away from the wavelengths at the peak of the initial spectra toward both longer and shorter wavelengths. Evolutions performed at different gravitational wave amplitudes are shown to produce cascades that scale with amplitude in the way predicted by the four-wave scattering models of gravitational-wave turbulence.

</details>

### [125] [On Lagrangians of Oakley-Usher type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15136)
*Joé Brendel,Andrea Piccirilli*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文研究非环面的Lagrangian子流形的辛（非）可结性，通过推广Oakley-Usher的广义Polterovich Lagrangian并适配非交换群作用的辛约化方法，发现Oakley-Usher Lagrangian与其（非）可结版本之间存在四种关系（非微分同胚、微分同胚但非Lagrangian同痕、Lagrangian同痕但非Hamilton同痕、Hamilton同痕），且紧化会改变这种关系。第二个主题是研究三维二次曲面Q³中的单调Lagrangian环面，通过计算Ψ-不变量的万有形变发现一个强可结环面，并证明了Oakley-Usher环面的不可位移性，进而通过Q³的单调Fukaya范畴的split-generation结果关联到镜像对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 推广非环面Lagrangian子流形的辛（非）可结性研究，填补此前仅关注环面情形的空白；2) 解决Oakley-Usher提出的关于Q³中特定环面不可位移性的公开问题；3) 通过Fukaya范畴的split-generation将几何结果与镜像对称联系起来。

Method: 1) 借鉴Oakley-Usher的广义Polterovich Lagrangian构造，将Chekanov-Schlenk和McDuff探针的辛约化方法适配到非交换群作用情形；2) 计算Shelukhin-Tonkonog-Vianna的Ψ-不变量的万有形变；3) 使用单调Fukaya范畴的split-generation技术。

Result: 1) 发现Oakley-Usher Lagrangian与其（非）可结版本之间存在四种层次的关系，且紧化会改变这些关系；2) 在Q³中发现一个强可结环面——不与任何Vianna环面的Biran提升Hamilton同痕；3) 证明了Oakley-Usher环面在Q³中的不可位移性；4) 证明了Q³的单调Fukaya范畴的split-generation结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了非环面Lagrangian子流形辛（非）可结性的系统分类框架，解决了Q³中环面不可位移性的公开问题，并通过镜像对称为Fukaya范畴的性质提供了新的理解。

Abstract: The first main theme of this paper is to study symplectic (un-)knottedness of Lagrangian submanifolds which are not tori. Our construction of such Lagrangians is inspired by the generalized Polterovich Lagrangians of Oakley--Usher and relies on an adaption of symplectic reduction methods going back to Chekanov--Schlenk and McDuff's probes to the case of non-abelian group actions. We compare Oakley--Usher Lagrangians to their (un-)knotted cousins to find that, depending on the situation, they are: (1) not diffeomorphic, (2) diffeomorphic, but not Lagrangian isotopic; (3) Lagrangian isotopic, but not Hamiltonian isotopic, (4) Hamiltonian isotopic. This relationship between them frequently changes when the ambient space is compactified.
  The second main theme is an in-depth study of monotone Lagrangian tori obtained from appyling our constructions to the three-dimensional quadric $Q^3$. Computing the versal deformation of the $Ψ$-invariant of Shelukhin--Tonkonog--Vianna, we find a torus which is knotted in a strong sense: it is not Hamiltonian isotopic to the Biran-lift of any Vianna torus in the two-dimensional quadric. Furthermore, we investigate enumerative properties of the Oakley--Usher torus in $Q^3$ and prove its non-displaceability, settling an open question asked by Oakley--Usher. We relate this to mirror symmetry by proving a split-generation result for the monotone Fukaya category of $Q^3$, which is inspired by, and can be compared to a similar result of Abouzaid--Diogo for cotangent bundles of the sphere.

</details>

### [126] [Quantum Markov Chains for an Asymmetric Mixed Ising-XY Model on a Cayley Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14343)
*Farrukh Mukhamedov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在半无限二阶根凯莱树上研究混合量子Ising-XY模型，使用树索引量子马尔可夫链的相容性准则，推导了平移不变边界方程并显式计算局部转移算子，证明该方程对任意参数存在唯一正解，从而模型存在唯一平移不变量子马尔可夫链，并分析了边界律动力学和局部两体纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合量子Ising-XY模型在树状结构（半无限二阶根凯莱树）上的边界条件与量子马尔可夫链的唯一性问题，探索量子统计力学中树晶格模型的相容性边界条件。

Method: 利用树索引量子马尔可夫链的相容性准则，结合归一化迹，推导平移不变边界方程，显式构造局部转移算子（单步部分迹映射），分析边界律参数的有限维递归动力学。

Result: 证明了边界方程对所有实耦合参数J_I, J_XY和任意β>0存在唯一正平移不变解；模型存在唯一平移不变量子马尔可夫链；边界律动力学无大于1的周期可容许点；计算了三体团簇上的局部两体纠缠。

Conclusion: 该混合量子Ising-XY模型在凯莱树上具有唯一的平移不变边界条件和量子马尔可夫链，边界律动力学无高阶周期点，为树晶格上量子自旋模型的严格分析提供了新结果。

Abstract: We study a mixed quantum Ising-$XY$ model on the semi-infinite rooted Cayley tree of order two. For every vertex $u$, the edge $\langle u,(u,1)\rangle$ carries an $XY$ interaction and the edge $\langle u,(u,2)\rangle$ carries an Ising interaction. Using the compatibility criterion for tree-indexed quantum Markov chains and consistently working with the normalized trace, we derive the translation-invariant boundary equation and compute explicitly the associated local transfer operator, namely the one-step partial-trace map which propagates successor boundary data to the parent vertex. We prove that the boundary equation has a unique positive translation-invariant solution for all $J_I,J_{XY}\in\mathbb R$ and $β>0$. Hence the model admits a unique translation-invariant quantum Markov chain generated by a positive translation-invariant boundary condition. We also show that the reduced boundary-law dynamics, i.e. the induced finite-dimensional recursion for the boundary-law parameters, has no admissible periodic points of period greater than one and compute the local two-site entanglement on the natural three-site cluster of the tree.

</details>


### [127] [On Factorizing Aggregate Counting Distributions into Independent Latent Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14409)
*Israel Klich*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究给定聚合计数变量的概率分布时，哪些独立潜在计数过程与该观测兼容的问题，即概率生成函数何时能分解为具有非负系数的归一化多项式。作者建立了正因分解的数学理论，包括正因分解偏序集、因分解熵、尖锐熵不等式等核心概念，并刻画了可因分解区域、实根与Hurwitz稳定扇区等几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从聚合计数变量的概率分布出发，反推其背后的独立潜在计数过程，这是一个逆问题。理解哪些独立结构可以生成观测到的聚合分布，对于统计物理、信息论和概率论中的潜在变量建模具有重要意义。

Method: 引入正因分解偏序集（元素为正因分解，按细化排序），定义因分解熵（与观测分布兼容的最大潜在香农熵）。利用概率生成函数的代数性质，分析尖锐熵不等式及其等号成立条件（潜在加法映射的单射性）。通过支持集障碍、实根与Hurwitz稳定扇区分类、局部稳定性定理、低次区域精确分类、四次Hurwitz体积计算及概率单纯形内的蒙特卡洛实验等工具进行系统研究。

Result: 证明了尖锐熵不等式并刻画了等号情形；表明熵优化可限制在最大原子分解上，并给出不同最大原子分解具有不同熵的例子；建立了基于支持集的正因分解障碍；完全刻画了实根与Hurwitz稳定扇区；证明了互质因分解的局部稳定性定理；精确确定了二、三次的可因分解区域；得到了四次Hurwitz体积的精确值；通过计算与蒙特卡洛实验探索了概率单纯形内可因分解区域的几何结构。

Conclusion: 正因分解偏序集是与概率生成函数相关的自然代数对象，为研究聚合计数统计中的潜在独立结构提供了理论框架。该理论在独立成分分析、潜在变量模型和统计物理等方向具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Given only the probability distribution of an aggregate counting variable, what independent latent counting processes are compatible with the observation? Equivalently, when does a probability-generating function admit a factorization into normalized polynomials with nonnegative coefficients? We develop a mathematical theory of such positive factorizations.
  We introduce the positive factorization poset, whose elements are all positive factorizations ordered by refinement, and define the factorization entropy, measuring the maximal latent Shannon entropy compatible with the observed distribution. We prove a sharp entropy inequality, characterize the equality case by injectivity of the latent addition map, show that entropy optimization may be restricted to maximal atomizations, and exhibit examples where distinct maximal atomizations have different entropy.
  We further establish support-based obstructions to positive factorization, characterize the real-rooted and Hurwitz-stable sectors, prove a local stability theorem for coprime factorizations, determine exactly the factorable regions in degrees two and three, obtain an exact quartic Hurwitz volume, and investigate the geometry of the factorable region inside the probability simplex through exact calculations and Monte Carlo experiments. These results identify the positive factorization poset as a natural algebraic object associated with probability-generating functions and provide a framework for studying latent independent structure in aggregate counting statistics.

</details>


### [128] [Polar Form of the Dirac Operator in Low-Dimensional Space-Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14752)
*Luca Fabbri,Giuseppe De Maria*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将Dirac算子以极坐标形式表示为左乘指定矩阵，并利用该技术寻找低维空间中的右预解式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了简化Dirac算子在低维空间中的分析，通过极坐标形式表达来构造其右预解式。

Method: 将Dirac算子表达为极坐标形式下的左乘指定矩阵操作，并基于此推导右预解式。

Result: 成功在低维空间中找到了Dirac算子的右预解式。

Conclusion: 极坐标形式的左乘矩阵表示是一种有效的技术，可用于推导Dirac算子的右预解式。

Abstract: We express in polar form the Dirac operator as left-multiplication by an assigned matrix: we use this technique to find its right-resolvent for low-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [129] [A C++ implementation of the G-Scheme stiff ODE solver with multi-resolution sparse hash-table kernel lookup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14918)
*Riccardo Malpica Galassi,Mauro Valorani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出cpp-gscheme，一个生产级C++实现的G-Scheme框架，利用多分辨率哈希表替代在线雅可比矩阵特征分解，在n-庚烷自点火机理测试中实现2.7x-9.3x加速比（相对于CVODE），成本缩放为N^1.4（CVODE为N^1.9），并能100%检索kernel集，零在线特征分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-Scheme的计算开销主要来自每时间步的雅可比矩阵特征系统（kernel集）评估。为降低此开销，作者先前提出了基于多分辨率稀疏哈希表的预计算查找策略（Python实现），本文旨在将该方法移植为生产级C++实现，保证行为等价性并提升性能。

Method: 采用多分辨率哈希表替换在线kernel计算，实现C++版本的G-Scheme框架；确保位级一致的检索钩子、相同的缩放/分箱/哈希语义，以及全原生化学源项评估路径。

Result: 在33个n-庚烷机理（56-459物种）及654物种详细机理上测试：100% kernel集检索率、零在线特征分解；加速比从2.7x（56物种）到9.3x（654物种）；成本缩放N^1.4（vs CVODE的N^1.9）；80物种骨架机理在5%点火延迟目标下达到225x加速比。

Conclusion: 求解器对总误差贡献可忽略，误差主要来自骨架缩减；多分辨率哈希策略可有效替代在线特征分解，显著提升刚性ODE系统求解效率。

Abstract: The G-Scheme is an explicit, adaptive integration framework for stiff systems of ordinary differential equations that exploits a local time-scale decomposition provided by the eigensystem of the Jacobian (the kernel set). Its computational cost is dominated by the evaluation of a new kernel set at each time step. In a recent paper [R.Malpica Galassi and M.Valorani, Combust. Theory Model. 2025], we introduced a multi-resolution sparse hash-table strategy that replaces online kernel computation with retrieval from a precomputed lookup table, demonstrated in Python. Here we present cpp-gscheme, a production-grade C++ implementation of both the G-Scheme and the multi-resolution hash table, together with the porting methodology that guarantees behavioural equivalence with the Python reference: identical scaling, binning and hashing semantics, a bitwise-inert retrieval hook in the integrator, and a fully native evaluation path for the chemical source terms. On 33 n-heptane autoignition mechanisms spanning 56-459 species plus the detailed 654-species mechanism, the hash-tabulated C++ G-Scheme retrieves 100 percent of the kernel sets, performs zero online eigen decompositions, and outperforms the natively compiled CVODE solver by a factor increasing from 2.7x (56 species) to 9.3x (654 species), with observed cost scaling of approximately N power 1.4 versus CVODE's N power 1.9. A three-metric accuracy assessment (ignition delay, equilibrium state, and accumulated entropy-progress error) shows that the solver contributes negligibly to the overall error budget, which is dominated by skeletal reduction. Consequently, a 5 percent ignition-delay target is achieved by the 80-species skeletal mechanism with a 225x speed-up over CVODE on the detailed mechanism. The software, training-set builders, and benchmark drivers are released as open source.

</details>


### [130] [Quasi-Pfaffian Solutions to Integrable Systems via Sylvester-Moutard Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15013)
*Claire R Gilson,Chen Shu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种名为quasiPfaffian的数学结构，它是非交换环境中替代Pfaffian的类似物，类似于quasideterminant。基于quasiPfaffian的Sylvester恒等式，发展了一种新型变换——Sylvester Moutard变换，可为Moutard可积系统（如Novikov Veselov方程和二维sine Gordon方程）生成新解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非交换代数环境中，传统的Pfaffian无法直接适用，需要一种类似quasideterminant的结构来替代Pfaffian，从而在非交换背景下处理与Pfaffian相关的可积系统问题。

Method: 定义了quasiPfaffian这一数学结构；推导并基于quasiPfaffian的Sylvester恒等式，构造了Sylvester Moutard变换；将经典Moutard变换置于quasiPfaffian框架下进行重新审视。

Result: 成功构造了Sylvester Moutard变换，该变换能够为Novikov Veselov方程和二维sine Gordon方程等Moutard可积系统生成新解。

Conclusion: quasiPfaffian是非交换环境中处理Pfaffian相关问题的有效工具，Sylvester Moutard变换是生成Moutard可积系统新解的有效方法，该结构还具有更多值得进一步探索的性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a mathematical structure called the quasiPfaffian. The quasiPfaffian is analogous to the quasideterminant, a structure used instead of a determinant in noncommutative settings. Building on the Sylvester identity for the quasiPfaffian, we develop a novel transformation, named the Sylvester Moutard transform, this generates new solutions for Moutard transformable integrable systems, such as the Novikov Veselov equation and the two dimensional sine Gordon equation. We also briefly review the classical Moutard transformation in the context of quasiPfaffians and discuss several additional properties of this new object.

</details>


### [131] [Study of Duffing oscillator using an improved Lindstedt Poincare method and relevant comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15233)
*Ramij Ahamed,Subhankar Ray*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Lindstedt-Poincare方法及其改进形式对无阻尼Duffing振子进行了全面分析，并与高阶Runge-Kutta数值解进行了比较。结果表明，本文提出的方法比标准LPM和Burton改进的LPM具有更好的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无阻尼Duffing振子作为一类重要的非线性动力系统，在物理、工程和生物系统中具有广泛应用，需要更精确的解析分析方法来研究其动力学行为。

Method: 采用Lindstedt-Poincare方法（LPM）及其修改形式进行分析，并与高阶Runge-Kutta数值解进行对比验证。

Result: 本文提出的改进方法在收敛性方面优于标准LPM和Burton改进的LPM。

Conclusion: 所提出的修改LPM方法能更有效地分析无阻尼Duffing振子，具有更好的收敛性能。

Abstract: The undamped Duffing oscillator is a nonlinear dynamical system with broad applications in physics, engineering and biological system. We present a comprehensive analysis of this system using the Lindstedt Poincare method (LPM) and its modifications and make comparison with numerical solution obtained using higher order Runge-Kutta. It is also shown the method suggested in this article converges better than the standard LPM and Lindstedt Poincare method with Burton's modification.

</details>


### [132] ["Goldfish'' equations for infinitely many particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15237)
*Francois Leyvraz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将"金鱼方程"（一组可通过多项式系数-零点变换精确求解的非线性常微分方程组）从有限N推广到无限N的可能性，主要困难在于从多项式到整函数的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将有限N的金鱼方程精确解推广到无限N的情况，以扩展该方法的适用范围。

Method: 通过将多项式系数到零点的变换推广到整函数领域，尝试将无限N情形下的金鱼方程变换为自由运动。

Result: 指出了主要困难在于从多项式到整函数的过渡，并提到非标准分析作为未来工作的备选方案。

Conclusion: 将金鱼方程解从有限N推广到无限N面临根本性数学困难，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: The ``goldfish'' equations, so named because of their striking beauty, are a system of $N$ nonlinear ODE's, where $N$ is an arbitrary integer. They can be solved exactly in a very simple manner, by transforming them to free motion using a transformation involving the transition from the set of {\em coefficients\/} of a polynomial to that of its {\em zeroes}. This paper aims to explore the possibility of extending this solution to the case in which $N$ is infinite. The main difficulty involves the transition from polynomials to entire functions. Another approach using non-standard analysis, is left to future work.

</details>


### [133] [A Geometric Derivation of the Einstein Equations from the Causal Action Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13871)
*Felix Finster,Christoph Krpoun*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了因果泛函系统的作用原理，证明在光滑流形支撑的极小化测度下，拉格朗日量诱导出洛伦兹度量，且欧拉-拉格朗日方程导致里奇张量满足广义相对论的爱因斯坦方程，其中引力耦合常数为正则化长度的平方，并提供了对爱因斯坦方程的修正推导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从因果变分原理和因果作用原理出发，推导出广义相对论的爱因斯坦方程，并建立引力耦合常数与正则化长度的关系，为引力理论的修正提供系统方法。

Method: 假设极小化测度的支撑为光滑流形$\tilde{M}$，引入振荡真空概念，通过拉格朗日量诱导洛伦兹度量，利用欧拉-拉格朗日方程推导里奇张量满足爱因斯坦方程，其中能量动量张量以正则化长度的幂展开表示。

Result: 成功推导出洛伦兹度量的存在性，证明爱因斯坦方程成立，引力耦合常数等于正则化长度的平方，并给出了系统修正爱因斯坦方程的程序。

Conclusion: 因果作用原理在光滑流形支撑下可以统一引力理论，为量子引力修正提供了新途径，并在四维时空下具体给出了洛伦兹设定。

Abstract: The causal action principle for causal fermion systems is analyzed for a minimizing measure whose support is assumed to have the structure of a smooth manifold $\tilde{M}$. The concept of osculating vacua is introduced. It is shown that the Lagrangian induces on $\tilde{M}$ a Lorentzian metric. Moreover, the Euler-Lagrange equations of the causal action imply that the Ricci tensor must satisfy the Einstein equations of general relativity for an energy momentum tensor given in terms of a power expansion in the regularization length. The gravitational coupling constant is found to be the square of the regularization length. Our methods provide a systematic procedure for deriving corrections to the Einstein equations.
  The paper includes a self-contained introduction to causal variational principles and the causal action principle. Most geometric structures (connection, Riemannian metric and curvature) are introduced and analyzed in the general setting of causal variational principles for an arbitrary dimension of $\tilde{M}$. The Lorentzian setting works only for causal fermion systems and is worked out only in four spacetime dimensions.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Harmonic Variables for Laplace Operators on Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14132)
*I. V. Shirokov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种黎曼和伪黎曼空间上拉普拉斯算子的调和变量定义，该变量可用于构造一类广泛非线性微分方程的特解（非不变群解），并针对具有双不变度量的李群及特定齐性空间（如对称空间）给出了构造此类变量的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非线性微分方程提供一种新的特解构造方法，通过调和变量突破传统不变群解的限制。

Method: 在黎曼/伪黎曼空间上定义调和变量，基于拉普拉斯算子的性质，并针对李群（双不变度量）和齐性空间（对称空间）设计具体构造算法。

Result: 成功引入调和变量定义，并给出了在双不变度量李群和对称空间等结构上的可操作构造算法。

Conclusion: 调和变量为非线性微分方程求解提供了有效工具，尤其适用于具有特殊几何结构的空间。

Abstract: A definition of harmonic variables for Laplace operators on Riemannian and pseudo-Riemannian spaces is introduced. Harmonic variables allow, in particular, the construction of particular (non-invariant group) solutions to a wide class of nonlinear differential equations. For Lie groups with a bi-invariant metric and a certain class of homogeneous spaces (e.g., symmetric spaces), an algorithm for constructing such variables is presented.

</details>


### [135] [A Self-Dual Frame Formalism of the SO(3) Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14204)
*Hanwen Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究闭定向黎曼4-流形上SO(3)联络的Yang-Mills方程，通过极分解将问题转化为正定对称矩阵场h的确定系统Φ_g(h)=0，证明其变分结构、不可约性、椭圆正则性及Fredholm指标定理，并给出与反自对偶及常数量曲率的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立闭定向黎曼4-流形上SO(3)联络Yang-Mills方程的一种新等价表述，将规范自由度去除后转化为关于正定对称矩阵场的确定偏微分方程系统，并探索其与反自对偶几何的深层联系。

Method: 利用极分解去除SO(3)联络的规范自由度，将自对偶曲率场转化为正定对称矩阵h；构造相容联络A(h)；将Yang-Mills方程等价转化为Φ_g(h)=0的二阶确定系统；建立变分原理并分析线性化算子的椭圆正则性和Fredholm指标。

Result: 证明了Φ_g(h)=0的变分公式化、自动不可约性、线性化算子的椭圆正则性与Fredholm指标定理；当h=e^{2ω}g时，该方程等价于反自对偶性加上常数量曲率6√2；每个正Yamabe不变量反自对偶共形类均给出整体解。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Yang-Mills理论与反自对偶4-流形几何之间的一种新联系，为研究SO(3)规范场提供了可计算的确定PDE框架，并揭示了反自对偶共形类与Yang-Mills方程解之间的对应关系。

Abstract: For a closed oriented Riemannian $4$-manifold $(M,g)$, we consider $\operatorname{SO}(3)$ connections on the bundle $Λ^+$ of self-dual $2$-forms. On the open locus where the self-dual curvature is an orientation-preserving frame, pointwise polar decomposition removes the gauge freedom and replaces the connection by a field $h$ of positive definite symmetric matrices. We show that $h$ determines a unique compatible connection $A(h)$ and that the Yang--Mills equation is equivalent to the exactly determined second order system $$ Φ_g(h):=F_{A(h)}^+h^{-1}-g=0. $$ We establish a variational formulation, automatic irreducibility, elliptic regularity, and a Fredholm index theorem for the linearized operator. For matrix fields of the form $h=e^{2ω}g$, the equation $Φ_g(h)=0$ is equivalent to anti-self-duality and constant scalar curvature $6\sqrt{2}$. Consequently, every anti-self-dual conformal class of positive Yamabe invariant gives a global solution.

</details>


### [136] [Scalar Curvature and Product Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14244)
*Claude LeBrun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文通过构造4维反例，证明当欧拉特征数为零时，Rosenberg的S^1-稳定性猜想（徐洁提出的限制形式）不成立：存在不具正数量曲率度量的光滑紧4流形M，但其与S^1的笛卡尔积却允许正数量曲率度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Rosenberg S^1-稳定性猜想在限制形式下的有效性，特别是针对徐洁提出的版本，通过寻找反例检验其边界条件。

Method: 构造欧拉特征数为零的4维光滑紧流形M，分别检验M及其与S^1的乘积流形M×S^1是否存在正数量曲率度量。

Result: 找到了不满足正数量曲率度量的4维流形M，但其与S^1的乘积却可以赋予正数量曲率度量，形成了反例。

Conclusion: Rosenberg S^1-稳定性猜想在限制形式下（徐洁提议的版本）不成立，欧拉特征数为零并非保证S^1乘积后正数量曲率存在的充分条件。

Abstract: We display 4-dimensional counter-examples to a restricted form of Rosenberg's S^1-Stability Conjecture recently proposed by Jie Xu. Namely, we show that there are smooth compact 4-manifolds M with Euler characteristic zero that do not admit positive-scalar-curvature Riemannian metrics, but for which the corresponding Cartesian products M x S^1 nevertheless do admit such metrics.

</details>


### [137] [Lipschitz continuity of the cut time for globally hyperbolic spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14374)
*Alec Metsch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在全局双曲时空中，焦散时间在未来因果锥的有限子集上局部Lipschitz连续，切割时间在包含切割点的切向量邻域上局部Lipschitz连续，并导出了零锥附近的Lipschitz常数定量估计，进而证明了点的切割轨迹具有至少1的Hausdorff余维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Riemann流形上Itoh-Tanaka和Li-Nirenberg关于切割时间的Lipschitz连续性经典结果推广到Lorentzian时空设定中。

Method: 采用Itoh-Tanaka的方法，并适当调整以适应Lorentzian几何框架。

Result: 建立了全局双曲时空中焦散时间和切割时间的局部Lipschitz连续性，给出了零锥附近Lipschitz常数的定量估计，证明了切割轨迹的Hausdorff余维数至少为1。

Conclusion: 将Riemann流形上的经典Lipschitz连续性结果成功拓展至Lorentzian时空，并获得了切割轨迹的维数性质。

Abstract: We prove that, for a fixed point in a globally hyperbolic spacetime, the focalization time is locally Lipschitz continuous on the open subset of the future causal cone where it is finite. We also show that the cut time is locally Lipschitz continuous in a neighborhood of any timelike tangent vector whose associated geodesic is defined at least up to (and including) its cut time. Furthermore, we derive quantitative estimates for the Lipschitz constant near the null cone and provide a criterion ensuring that the Lipschitz property extends to null directions. As a consequence, we show that the cut locus of a point has Hausdorff codimension at least $1$. These results extend classical Lipschitz continuity results for complete Riemannian manifolds due to Itoh-Tanaka and Li-Nirenberg. Our approach follows the method of Itoh-Tanaka, suitably adapted to the Lorentzian setting.

</details>


### [138] [The existence of $k$-convex hypersurface for a class of Hessian curvature equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14717)
*Kang Xiao,Jiabao Gong*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Hessian曲率方程封闭星形超曲面的存在性，通过先验估计与连续性方法，建立了非齐次和齐次Hessian曲率方程k-凸超曲面的存在唯一性，并利用常数秩定理证明了所得k-凸超曲面是严格凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一类Hessian曲率方程中封闭星形超曲面的存在性，这是偏微分方程与微分几何交叉领域的重要问题。

Method: 采用先验估计与连续性方法相结合，并建立常数秩定理。

Result: 建立了非齐次和齐次Hessian曲率方程k-凸超曲面的存在唯一性，并证明了这些k-凸超曲面的严格凸性。

Conclusion: 所研究的Hessian曲率方程存在封闭星形k-凸超曲面解，且这些解具有严格凸的几何性质。

Abstract: This article investigates the existence of closed, star-shaped hypersurfaces for a class of Hessian curvature equations. By combining a priori estimates with the continuity method, we establish the existence and uniqueness of $k$-convex hypersurfaces for both nonhomogeneous and homogeneous Hessian curvature equations, and by establishing a constant rank theorem, we prove that the resulting $k$-convex hypersurfaces are strictly convex.

</details>


### [139] [A framework for producing harmonic maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14857)
*Volker Branding,Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于从固定调和映射生成从单位球到欧几里得球面的一族调和映射，为Nakauchi最近引入的广义径向投影提供了理论背景，并展示了这些调和映射族如何用于构造相关变分问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广义径向投影提供理论背景，并推广Toth关于特征映射的生成机制，将其应用于调和映射的构造。

Method: 基于Toth的方法，从给定的调和映射出发，通过框架化生成一族新的调和映射。

Result: 建立了一个生成从单位球到欧几里得球面的调和映射族的通用框架，为Nakauchi的广义径向投影提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 这些调和映射族可用于构造内蕴双调和映射、三调和映射、p-调和映射以及外在多调和映射等变分问题的解。

Abstract: We present a general framework for producing families of harmonic maps from the unit ball into the Euclidean sphere starting from a fixed harmonic map. In particular, our analysis provides a theoretical background for a generalized radial projection which was recently introduced by Nakauchi.
  Our approach is based on work of Toth who established a machinery that generates eigenmaps between spheres from a given eigenmap.
  Finally, we point out how these families of harmonic maps can be used to construct solutions to associated variational problems such as intrinsic biharmonic and triharmonic maps, $p$-harmonic maps as well as extrinsic polyharmonic maps.

</details>


### [140] [A quantitative Schur comparison theorem for curves in CAT(k) spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15106)
*Mohammad Ghomi,John Ioannis Stavroulakis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在CAT(k)空间中建立了有限总曲率曲线的Schur比较定理的定量形式，通过引入曲率测度和力学中的力臂概念，给出了模型平面上的比较公式，并改进了Reshetnyak定理，从而推广了欧氏空间中的经典弓和弓臂引理及其黎曼类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schur比较定理在欧氏空间和黎曼流形中已有广泛应用，但缺乏对CAT(k)空间中有限总曲率曲线的定量刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，为更一般的非正曲率空间提供精确的比较工具。

Method: 1) 引入曲率测度和力学中力臂的概念，建立模型平面上的比较公式；2) 改进Reshetnyak定理，更好地控制majorizing曲线的曲率；3) 结合上述工具推导出CAT(k)空间中Schur比较定理的定量形式。

Result: 获得了CAT(k)空间中有限总曲率曲线的Schur比较定理的定量版本，该结果精简并扩展了欧氏空间中的经典arm和bow引理及其黎曼类比。

Conclusion: 本文建立的定量Schur比较定理为CAT(k)空间中的曲线几何提供了新的分析工具，其证明方法将曲率测度、力学概念与几何比较融为一体，具有方法论意义。

Abstract: We obtain a quantitative form of Schur's comparison theorem for curves with finite total curvature in CAT(k) spaces. This sharpens and extends the classical arm and bow lemmas in Euclidean space, as well as their Riemannian analogues. The proof is based on a comparison formula for curves in model planes, expressed in terms of curvature measures and a notion of moment arm borrowed from mechanics. Another ingredient is a refinement of Reshetnyak's theorem that controls the curvature of the majorizing curve.

</details>


### [141] [On the Darboux Integrability of the Constant Mean Curvature One Equation for Surfaces in Hyperbolic 3 space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15244)
*Mark E. Fels,Thomas A. Ivey*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文通过群论构造，证明了决定双曲三维空间中常平均曲率浸入的椭圆偏微分方程组等价于椭圆Liouville方程。特别地，每个方程的Darboux可积性表明这些方程存在等价的商表示，并最终归结为方程的等价性。文中给出了实现等价性的显式映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立常平均曲率浸入系统与已知可积系统（Liouville方程）之间的等价关系，从而利用Liouville方程的理论来理解和求解常平均曲率问题。

Method: 利用群论构造，分析椭圆型偏微分方程组的结构性质，通过Darboux可积性理论建立方程之间的商表示，并构造显式的等价映射。

Result: 成功证明了描述双曲三维空间中常平均曲率浸入的椭圆方程组与椭圆Liouville方程之间的等价性，并给出了具体的显式映射。

Conclusion: 常平均曲率浸入问题可归结为椭圆Liouville方程，后者丰富的可积性理论可用于前者问题的研究。

Abstract: We show, using group theoretic constructions, that the system of elliptic partial differential equations whose solutions determine the constant mean curvature immersions into hyperbolic 3 space are equivalent to the elliptic Liouville equation. In particular, the Darboux integrability of each equation shows that each of these equations admit an equivalent quotient representation which descends to an equivalence of the equations. The explicit map providing the equivalence is given.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Candia-v2: Logarithmic expansions for DGLAP evolution in $x$-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14151)
*Casey Hampson,Marco Guzzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Candia-v2是一个开源软件包，将x-space Candia算法推广到QCD的N³LO精度，用于求解非极化核子部分子密度的DGLAP演化方程，相较于原始版本显著提升了效率和API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Candia算法从现有的精度扩展到N³LO（次次次领头阶），以匹配当前高能物理实验对理论精度的需求。

Method: 采用高效的对数展开技术，系统化地将算法扩展到QCD的所有阶次；以开源方式重新实现并改进原有C语言代码。

Result: 实现了N³LO精度的DGLAP演化求解器，效率显著提升，API更友好，并在GitHub上以GPLv3许可证公开。

Conclusion: Candia-v2为高精度QCD计算提供了可靠、高效且可扩展的工具，超越了原始C实现。

Abstract: We present Candia-v2, an open-source software package that generalizes the $x$-space Candia algorithm to next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N$^{3}$LO) accuracy in Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD). The code solves the DGLAP evolution equations for unpolarized nucleon parton densities using a highly efficient logarithmic expansion technique that can be systematically extended to all orders in QCD. Candia-v2 supersedes the previous original C implementation of the algorithm with significantly increased efficiency and improved API. The software is publicly available on GitHub under the GPLv3 license.

</details>


### [143] [New approach to the estimation of the lowest boundary of an axion mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14156)
*Sevinj O. Huseynova,Vali A. Huseynov,Nigar E. Musazade,Sevinj Y. Rzayeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析电子在均匀磁场中的能谱（考虑反常磁矩），并结合氢原子超精细结构能级间的三重态-单态跃迁，理论估算了轴子质量的最低边界——大于5.885微电子伏特，并推测太阳黑子中电子发射的轴子对应质量约10微电子伏特量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子是强CP问题的重要候选解，但质量尚不确定。为约束轴子质量的下限，需要结合具体的物理系统和跃迁过程进行理论估算。

Method: 1. 求解电子在恒定均匀磁场中考虑反常磁矩后的能谱；2. 将该结果应用于氢原子中处于质子库仑场及质子磁矩产生的磁场中的电子；3. 利用氢原子s-能级超精细结构分裂中相邻能级间的三重态-单态跃迁，对轴子质量下限施加约束。

Result: 轴子质量下限为5.885微电子伏特；轴子发射所需最小磁场强度由轴子质量决定；推测太阳黑子中电子发射的轴子质量约10微电子伏特量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为轴子质量提供了明确的理论下限，并建立了轴子发射与磁场强度的关系，有助于指导实验探测方向。

Abstract: In this work, we have performed the theoretical estimation of the lowest boundary of an axion mass. We have analyzed the energy spectrum of the electron in a constant homogeneous magnetic field, taking into account its anomalous magnetic moment. We have applied the obtained results on the energy spectrum of the electron in a constant homogeneous magnetic field with allowance for its anomalous magnetic moment to the hydrogen atom electron, which is located in addition to the Coulomb field of the proton, as well as in the magnetic field produced by the magnetic moment of the proton. We have applied the idea of the restriction for the lowest boundary of the axion mass to the triplet-singlet transition between two adjacent levels obtained as a result of the hyperfine structure splitting of the s-levels in the hydrogen atom. We have concluded that the mass of an axion ought to be more than 5.885 microelectron-Volt. We have determined that the minimum value of the magnetic field strength at which the axion emission by the electron in a magnetic field starts to be realized is determined by the axion mass. This result enables us to estimate the expected value of the lowest boundary for the axion mass (of the order of 10 microelectron-Volt), assuming that some part of the solar axions is emitted by the electrons located in sunspots.

</details>


### [144] [Dark matter in composite Higgs models with a scotogenic EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14210)
*Yu Chen,Werner Porod*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有费米子UV完备化的复合希格斯模型，该模型能够解释暗物质 relic 密度。其低能理论类似于所谓的 scotogenic 模型。一个剩余的 Z₂ 对称性使得其中一个伪南部-戈德斯通玻色子稳定，从而成为可行的暗物质候选者。以 SU(6)/Sp(6) 陪集模型为例，理论上存在四个暗物质候选者，但仅三个能产生与观测一致的 relic 密度。通过 MCMC 方法研究暗物质可观测量，探索有效理论的参数空间，发现自旋-1共振在大部分参数空间中起重要作用。最后讨论了该模型在LHC上的可能信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能够同时解释电弱对称性破缺和暗物质 relic 密度的复合希格斯模型，并为其提供费米子UV完备化理论框架。

Method: 基于 SU(6)/Sp(6) 陪集的复合希格斯模型，利用剩余 Z₂ 对称性保证暗物质稳定性；通过 MCMC 研究对暗物质可观测量进行参数空间扫描分析。

Result: 该模型中有四个候选暗物质粒子，但仅三个能产生与观测一致的 relic 密度；自旋-1共振在大部分参数空间中显著影响暗物质物理。

Conclusion: 此类复合希格斯模型能够有效解释暗物质 relic 密度，并可在LHC上给出可探测的信号特征。

Abstract: We discuss a class of Composite Higgs models with a fermionic UV completion that can explain the dark matter relic density. The resulting low-energy theory resembles so-called scotogenic models. A residual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry implies that one of the pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone bosons is stable and therefore is a viable dark matter candidate. As a concrete example, we take a model based on the $\mathrm{SU}(6)/\mathrm{Sp}(6)$ coset, which in principle contains four dark matter candidates. However, only three of them can produce a relic density consistent with observations. We perform a MCMC study focusing on dark matter observables to explore the available parameter space of the effective theory. In particular, we find that spin-1 resonances play an important role in a substantial part of the parameter space. Finally, we comment on possible LHC signatures of this model class.

</details>


### [145] [Searching for the $N$-naturalness tower of neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14243)
*Sofia Lonardi,Manuel Ettengruber,Philipp Eller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次利用全球中微子数据对N-自然性模型中的中微子塔进行实验搜索。通过排除参数空间(r, N)，发现当N≤10^4且r≥0.1时，马约拉纳中微子情况被排除，这意味大统一理论基准场景被中微子实验否定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-自然性模型作为层级问题的一个潜在解决方案，需要实验检验。该模型预测存在N个暗扇区，其中希格斯质量参数变化，并通过参数r编码扇区放置所需的精细调谐。目前缺乏对模型中微子塔的实验约束。

Method: 利用公开中微子数据进行全局分析，对N-自然性模型的中微子塔进行实验搜索。分别考虑正常序和反序、马约拉纳中微子和狄拉克中微子两种情况，对参数空间(r, N)给出排除限。

Result: 对于马约拉纳中微子情况，可以排除N≤10^4个扇区且r≥0.1的参数区域。特别地，N=10^4（大统一理论基准场景）且无精细调谐的情况被排除。

Conclusion: 中微子实验对N-自然性模型给出了强约束，排除了大统一理论框架下的基准场景，表明该模型可能需要在参数空间中进行额外调整。

Abstract: We present the first experimental search for the $N$-naturalness tower of neutrinos using a global analysis of publicly available neutrino data. As a potential solution to the hierarchy problem, the $N$-naturalness model employs a varying Higgs mass parameter among $N$ dark sectors and encodes the required fine-tuning of the placement of the sectors in a parameter $r$. We report exclusion limits on the parameter space ($r$, $N$), for normal and inverted ordering and in case the neutrino is Majorana or Dirac. In the case of a Majorana neutrino, we can rule out $N\leq 10^4$ sectors with $r\geq 0.1$. This is particularly exciting as it shows that a gauge and gravitational unification around $M_{GUT}$, which is a benchmark scenario of the $N$-naturalness framework with $N=10^4$ without fine-tuning, is ruled out by neutrino experiments.

</details>


### [146] [Constraining the Coexistence of Primordial Black Holes and Particle Dark Matter with Neutrino Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14253)
*Prolay Chanda,Sagnik Mukherjee,James Unwin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了原初黑洞（PBH）与粒子暗物质共存时，从高能中微子观测角度对二者的约束。通过精细处理暗物质晕轮廓和红移演化，考虑冻结、冻结-输入及玻尔兹曼抑制冻结-输入模型，利用IceCube事件灵敏度和弥散通量上限给出了限制，并在U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}规范中介模型下讨论了带电轻子与中微子关联末态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对PBH与粒子暗物质共存的约束不够精确，尤其是在暗物质晕轮廓处理和红移演化方面。本文旨在通过高能中微子观测，更严格地约束两者共存参数空间。

Method: 1) 精细处理PBH周围暗物质晕（minispikes）的密度轮廓及随红移的演化；2) 考虑三种暗物质产生机制：冻结（freeze-out）、冻结-输入（freeze-in）和玻尔兹曼抑制冻结-输入；3) 利用IceCube事件级灵敏度和弥散通量上限两种方式设置约束；4) 在U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}规范中介模型下讨论带电轻子与中微子关联末态。

Result: 给出了不同暗物质模型下，PBH质量与暗物质粒子参数空间中被中微子观测排除的区域；指出在U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}模型中，由于带电轻子末态的存在，实际约束比纯中微子模型更弱。

Conclusion: 高能中微子观测能有效约束PBH与粒子暗物质的共存参数空间，且完整的粒子物理模型（如U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}）必须考虑末态带电轻子对约束的稀释效应。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBH) with a uniform mass scale could contribute up to 1\% of the gravitationally inferred dark matter relic abundance and remain consistent with observational limits over a large range of masses. In this case, the vast majority of the dark matter relic abundance is comprised of dark matter particles, such as WIMPs or FIMPs. Particle dark matter gravitationally captured around primordial black holes can form dense minispikes in which the annihilation rate is strongly enhanced. In this work, we investigate the constraints on the coexistence of PBHs and particle dark matter from high-energy neutrino observations. Relative to earlier analyses, we refine the treatment of the dark matter halo profile and its redshift evolution. We consider models of freeze-out and freeze-in dark matter, as well as Boltzmann-suppressed freeze-in. We present idealized IceCube event-based sensitivities together with conservative limits obtained by requiring that the predicted extragalactic neutrino intensity not exceed the upper envelope of the measured diffuse flux. We explore the constraints in terms of an idealized model with 100\% branching to neutrinos, we also discuss these results within the context fo a motivated gauged U(1)${}_{L_μ-L_τ}$ mediator model, emphasizing that a consistent particle-physics completion generally predicts correlated charged-lepton and neutrino final states.

</details>


### [147] [Collinear Factorization Violation and Reggeization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14259)
*Damiano Barcaro,Anjie Gao,Jonathan R. Gaunt,Aditya Pathak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在类光共线极限下胶子位于Glauber区域时的全阶软共线子图因子化，证明了这些贡献表现出Regge化特性，并通过避开Glauber区域极大简化了有效理论子图，从而降低了多点振幅中共线因子化破坏项的计算复杂度，同时开发了新的软件工具用于多圈水平上的Glauber交换系统研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单胶子位于Glauber区域时振幅的共线因子化性质，特别是探索Regge化行为以及简化共线因子化破坏项的计算。

Method: 推导了空间类共线极限下的全阶软共线子图因子化，通过避开Glauber区域对有效理论子图进行简化，并开发了新的软件工具辅助多圈水平的系统研究。

Result: 证实了Glauber区域贡献表现出Regge化，结合了前向散射和硬散射的特征；通过避开Glauber区域实现了有效理论子图的显著简化，大幅降低了多点振幅中共线因子化破坏项的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文为系统研究涉及Glauber交换的多圈过程提供了理论框架和软件工具，有效简化了共线因子化破坏项的计算。

Abstract: We derive an all-order soft-collinear subgraph factorization in the space-like collinear limits of amplitudes for single gluon pinched in the Glauber region. We show that these contributions exhibit Reggeization, combining aspects of both forward and hard scattering. By deforming away from the Glauber region wherever possible we demonstrate a dramatic simplification of the effective theory subgraphs and thereby drastically reducing the complexity in computing collinear factorization breaking terms in multi-point amplitudes. Our work is aided with development of new software tools initiating a systematic study of processes involving Glauber exchanges at a multi-loop level.

</details>


### [148] [Production Effects and Final-state Interactions in $π_1 \to 3π$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14300)
*D. Winney,S. Gonzàlez-Solís,M. Mikhasenko,Ł. Bibrzycki,C. Fernández-Ramírez,V. Mathieu,G. Montaña,A. Pilloni,L. Qiu,A. Rodas,A. P. Szczepaniak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Khuri-Treiman形式主义统一了产生效应和末态相互作用，对COMPASS合作组在π₁(1600)信号的3π道（J^PC=1⁻⁺, P波）中“自由同位旋”技术得到的数据进行全局拟合，提取了短程产生和Deck机制的贡献，并给出了3π不变质量依赖关系，为未来提取π₁在ρπ道的极点位置奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS合作组通过“自由同位旋”技术观测到π₁(1600)信号中P波ππ子道的线形修正，但这些修正同时来自于末态相互作用和产生过程（如Deck机制），现有分析未能统一描述这两种效应，因此需要建立一个兼顾低能幺正性、解析性和交叉对称性的理论框架来分离并量化不同产生机制的贡献。

Method: 采用Khuri-Treiman形式主义，该形式主义与低能幺正性、解析性和交叉对称性自洽，并以初始产生振幅为种子；对COMPASS的π₁自由同位旋数据集进行全局拟合，得到π₁→3π衰变振幅的多维参数化（同时依赖衰变变量和产生变量）。

Result: 成功识别并分离了短程产生和Deck机制的贡献，提取了总3π不变质量依赖关系，得到了π₁→3π衰变振幅的多维参数化描述。

Conclusion: 该统一形式主义能够有效区分不同产生机制对ππ质谱线形的贡献，所得到的3π不变质量依赖关系对将来在ρπ道中提取π₁极点位置具有重要价值。

Abstract: We investigate the $π_1(1600)$ signal channel of $3π$ in the $J^{PC} = 1^{-+} \,[ππ]_{1^{--}}π~P$-wave. This channel has recently been analyzed by the COMPASS collaboration using the ``freed-isobar" technique which provides direct experimental access to the lineshape modifications of the $P$-wave $ππ$ sub-channel arising from both final state interactions and production processes such as the Deck mechanism. We motivate a unified formalism combining production effects and final state interactions using the Khuri-Treiman formalism, which is consistent with low-energy unitarity, analyticity, and crossing symmetry, seeded by an initial production amplitude. We demonstrate that the precise lineshape of the $ππ$ mass spectrum can be used to determine the relative strength and complex phase of different production mechanisms contributing to the $3π$ final state. We conduct a global fit of the $π_1$ freed-isobar data set, yielding a multi-dimensional parameterization of the $π_1\to3π$ decay amplitude at COMPASS as a function of both decay and production variables. We identify the contributions from short-range production and the Deck mechanism and extract the total $3π$ invariant mass dependence which will be important for a future extraction of the $π_1$ pole position in the $ρπ$ channel.

</details>


### [149] [Exotic states with charm and/or strangeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14389)
*A. Martínez Torres,Breno Agatão,Pedro Brandão,K. P. Khemchandani,Luciano M. Abreu,E. Oset*

TL;DR: 该讲座讨论了几种含粲和/或奇异数的奇特态的性质，这些态可解释为来自两体或三体强子动力学，特别关注D₁(2420)及从n\bar获得的三体态预测。
